# Rule coverage audit

- Sources: **970**
- Rule strings: **22220**
- No errors on dummy input: **805 / 970 (83.0%)**
- Blocked sources: **165**
- Executed rule fields: **20034**
- Skipped metadata fields: **2186**

Input types are inferred from source metadata. Every rule is dry-run against both the HTML and the JSON dummy payload, and only counts as failing when neither of them lets it execute — a rule that merely needs a key the dummy does not carry is an artefact, not an engine gap. That fallback is not accepted as evidence when the preferred dialect could not even *parse* the rule: there it can only report an empty match. Empty matches count as successful execution; this report does not establish extraction correctness or online availability. JSON field paths and display templates are executed by the production engine. Failure locations use zero-based corpus array indexes.

1; j++) {<br>            let otherItem = str.charAt(j);<br>            if (!/[\d&#124;\.]/.test(otherItem)) {<br>              break;<br>            }<br>          }<br>          num = str.slice(i, j);<br>          i = j - 1;<br>          num = +num;<br>      }<br>      arr.push(num);<br>   }<br>   return arr;<br>}<br>// arr 即为上一步处理好的数组<br>function countHandle(arr) {<br>      let charArr = [],<br>        numArr = [];<br>      for (let i = 0; i &lt; arr.length; i++) {<br>        if (typeof arr[i] == 'number') {<br>          numArr.push(arr[i]);<br>        } else {<br>          if (charArr.length) {<br>            // 步骤1<br>            // 如果当前的运算符的优先级比栈顶的优先级低或相等，就说明需要把前面的值全部计算好<br>            // 存储运算符的栈要一直出栈，直到栈为空或当前的字符的优先级比栈顶的优先级高<br>            while (this.isPop(arr[i], charArr[charArr.length - 1])) {<br>              let t2 = numArr.pop();<br>              let t1 = numArr.pop();<br>              let char = charArr.pop();<br>              this.handleCalculation(numArr, t1, t2, char);<br>            }<br>            // 当前运算符为右括号<br>            if (arr[i] == ')') {<br>              // 取栈顶运算符<br>              let st = charArr[charArr.length - 1];<br>              // 步骤2<br>              // 遇到右括号也要一直出栈，直到遇到左括号<br>              while (st != '(') {<br>                let t1, t2;<br>                let char = charArr.pop();<br>                if (char != '(') {<br>                  t2 = numArr.pop();<br>                  t1 = numArr.pop();<br>                  this.handleCalculation(numArr, t1, t2, char);<br>                }<br>                st = char;<br>              }<br>            }<br>            // 运算符不为右括号<br>            if (arr[i] != ')') {<br>              charArr.push(arr[i]);<br>            }<br>          } else {<br>            // 步骤3<br>            // 运算符栈为空，直接入栈<br>            charArr.push(arr[i]);<br>          }<br>        }<br>      }<br>      // 步骤4<br>      // 最后运算符栈如果还有字符，要一直出栈直到为空<br>      while (charArr.length) {<br>        let t2 = numArr.pop();<br>        let t1 = numArr.pop();<br>        let char = charArr.pop();<br>        this.handleCalculation(numArr, t1, t2, char);<br>      }<br>   return numArr[0];<br>}<br>// 基本加减乘除运算处理<br>    function handleCalculation(numArr, num1, num2, char) {<br>      if (char == '+') {<br>        numArr.push(num1 + num2);<br>      } else if (char == '-') {<br>        numArr.push(num1 -
